Boats arriving from .Tangyang pay full inward dues if discharging at Shasi town, local trade dues if dischargin near the West station; but in the Shipping table they are all entered as local traders. In the same way boats from Shasi town to Tangyang pay full outward dues, but are classed among local clearances. Boats arriving from Tangyan* for other ports pay full outward dues. They are entered in the Shiping table first as local entries, then as intet clearances. Boats arriving from other ports for Tangyang have usuay no dues to pay, but in the Shipping table ty are entered, firstly, as interport entries and, secondly, as local clearances. II.-IMPORTS. 1*. PART IV. NOTIFICATIONS, During -the Quarter ended March 31, 1910.
264 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [JAN.-MAR., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 482. FOOCHOW DISTRICT. ENTRANCE TO MIN RIVER. POSITIONS OF Buoys. NOTICE is hereby given that the positions of buoys marking the entrance to the Min River are follows:- East Channel Entrance Buoy: from the buoy, Rees Rock bears S. 82* W., distant 3.74 miles. Entrance Shoal Buoy: from the buoy, Rees Rock bears S. 63* W., distant 2.04 miles. Middle Buoy: from the buoy, Rees Rock bears $. 390 W., distant 1.47 miles. Inner Buoy : from the buoy, Rees Rock bears S. 28&#39; E., distant 1.5 miles. The positions of the banks relatively to the buoys and the depth of water in the channel are at present not known with sufficient accuracy to enable directions for entering the river to be given. In the absence of local knowledge, a pilot should be taken. SHANGHAI, I8th February 1910. No. 483. SWATOW DISTRICT. TEMPORARY LIGHT ON CHILANG ROCK. NOTIcE is hereby given that on or about the ist April a temporary light will be exhibited from the summit of Chilang Rock (Chilang Point). The light will be group-occulting, of the sixth order, showing a white light varied by three occultations in quick succession every 30 seconds, thus.- Light . . . . . . . . . . . . 20 seconds Eclipse.... ......... 2 Light... .......... ... 2 Eclipse .... ......... 2 Light.. 2....... 2 Eclipse.... ...... . 2 . and so on.
1910.] NOTIFICATIONS. 2o5 The light, which will be elevated about 120 feet above the level of the sea, should be visible in clear weather at a distance of io nautical miles. Approximate position:- Latitude . . . . . . . . . . . 220 39&#39; N. Longitude . . . . . . . . . 15&#39; 34&#39; E. This light is established for the service of vessels attending the construction of a first order light-station on Chilang Rock. SHANGHI, 8th March 19o. No. 484. ALTERATION IN CHARACTER OF FOG-GUN SIGNALS. NOTIcE is hereby given that, from the under-mentioned dates, the character of the responding fog-gun signals from light-stations will be altered as described in the following instructions to Lightkeepers:- &quot;The keeper oh hearing a bell, fog-horn, steam whistle or siren, or any other sound, during foggy or thick weather, which shall indicate the proximity of a vessel, will immediately fire one gun, and after a lapse of five minutes will fire two guns with the. station&#39;s distinguishing interval between them; and this distinguishing signal of two guns will be repeated every five minutes, if the vessel&#39;s fog signal- showing that she is under way-continues to be heard.&quot; The distinguishing intervals of the several light-stations and the dates from which the change will be effected are- From the 15th April 1910. Steep Island ..... . .. . . . . . 2 minutes. West Volcano . . . . . . . . . . . . i minute. Bonham Island .i... . . .. . . 10 seconds. South-west Horn. . . . . . . . . 30, Gutzlaff . . . . . . . . . . 2 minutes. North Saddle. . . . . . . ... .. . . 30 seconds. Shaweishan . . . . . . . . .. . . I minute. North-east Shantung Promontory (in the event of the fog-siren being disabled) . . . . . I Kungtungtao . . . . . . . . . . . 2 minutes. Howki.. .......... . . . . . I minute.
266 CUSTOMS GAZBTTE. [JAN.-MAR. 1910.1 From the 1st Tuly 1910. Breaker Point . . . . . . . . . . . . I minute. Cape of Good Hope . . . . . . . . . io seconds. Lamocks . . . . . . . . . . .. . . . 2 minutes. Chapel Island...... . . . . . . . I minute. Tsingseu . .. . . . . . . . . . . . . 2 minutes. Dodd Island . . . . . . . . . . . . io seconds. Ockseu. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 2 minutes. Turnabout . . . . . . . . . . . . . I minute. Middle Dog . . . . . . . . . . . . . io seconds. Tungyung . . . . . . . . . . . . . 2 minutes. It should be noted that the first signal made by all stations will be ONE gun; that FIVE minutes later a signal of TWO guns will be made, the interval between which will enable the mariner to determine from which of the group of light-stations in his neighbourhood the signal has been made. The establishment of First Order Fog-siren Stations at Steep Island and Turnabout will be commenced during the year. SHANGHAI, 318t March iolo.
